# Commands · R · part 12

1,375 entries, research to resource-views. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **research**: Conduct a multi-database scientific research investigation inline in the current chat. Same operating discipline as the /tooluniverse:researcher agent (look up specific claims, cross-validate, honest INDETERMINATE) — but runs in this conversation so you can watch each step, follow up, and refine, instead of receiving a single summary from a forked subagent. Use when you want to drive the research yourself with ToolUniverse rather than delegating to a subagent. ([mims-harvard/ToolUniverse](https://github.com/mims-harvard/ToolUniverse/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/research.md) · 2 copies)

- **reset**: `libra reset` 的目标是移动 HEAD、索引和工作区到指定状态，覆盖 hard/mixed/soft/merge/keep 与 pathspec reset（含 `--pathspec-from-file` / `--pathspec-file-nul` / `--no-refresh`），并提供结构化输出。`--merge`/`--keep` 以完整预检与精确 snapshot rollback 保护本地变更。`reset -p` 的 Unstage 与 Apply 两种模式复用 `src/internal/patch_mode/`：对 HEAD/`@` 反向撤销暂存，对其它 tree-ish 把反向 diff 应用到索引；HEAD 与工作树不写。 ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/reset.md) · ★101)
